        "title": "Baidu Netdisk - AList Docs",
        "description": "# Baidu Netdisk\n\n[Andy Hsu](https://i.nn.ci) September 7, 2022 Guide Storage Guide 302 Official About 5 min\n\n* * *\n\nOn This Page\n\n* Refresh token\n* Storage Update\n* Root folder path\n* Custom crack ua\n* Download api\n* Add \"User-Agent\" usage example\n* Upload Config\n* The default download method used\n\n# Baidu Netdisk\n\nTips\n\nSupported version:\n\n* `Use dynamic upload api` and improved upload fallback / retry: `>= v3.56.0`\n\nTips\n\nDue to the limitation of Baidu Disk API, downloading files larger than about 20M needs to carry the header: \"User-Agent\": \" [pan.baidu.com](http://pan.baidu.com) \", so when downloading files larger than 20M, you need to set the request header yourself, such as using curl:\n\n```\ncurl  -L  -X  GET  'YOUR_LINK'  -H  'User-Agent: pan.baidu.com'\n```\n\nOr use the proxy function in this program to transfer.\n\n## **Refresh token**\n\n[Click here](https://openapi.baidu.com/oauth/2.0/authorize?response_type=code&client_id=hq9yQ9w9kR4YHj1kyYafLygVocobh7Sf&redirect_uri=https://alistgo.\ncom/tool/baidu/callback&scope=basic,netdisk&qrcode=1) to get the refresh token.\n\n## **Storage Update**\n\nThe Baidu Netdisk client ID and secret have been updated. You only need to update these in your AList storage settings to restore normal mounting and usage of Baidu Netdisk. Please refer to the image below for detailed instructions.\n\n* Client ID: hq9yQ9w9kR4YHj1kyYafLygVocobh7Sf\n* Client Secret: YH2VpZcFJHYNnV6vLfHQXDBhcE7ZChyE  \n  alist\n\n## **Root folder path**\n\nThe root foler to mount, defaults to `/`\n\n* Mount a folder separately, according to the following format, `/` is the root directory, just extend to that directory if you want to mount that directory\n  \n    + /folder\\_A/....../folder\\_x\n\n  \n## ~~**Custom crack ua**~~\n\n~~[**UA used when using 【Native Proxy & Crack API】**](https://github.com/alist-org/alist/issues/5602)~~ The unofficial interface is no longer available\n\n  \n## **Download api**\n\n...\n\n+ The browser also needs to modify the UA: `pan.baidu.com` or `netdisk` In short, if it is not the browser User-Agent, the video can be played.\n    + The specific situation is the same as the previous unofficial interface usage\n    + The continuous use time is unknown, and 100% availability is not guaranteed\n\n```\n{ \terror_code :  31119 , \terror_msg :  \"hit black userlist , hit illeage dlna\" , \trequest_id :  541111111111111140 }, { \terror_code :  31329 , \terror_msg :  \"hit black userlist , hit illeage dlna\" , \trequest_id :  921111381111111100 }\n```\n\n  \n## **Add \"User-Agent\" usage example**\n\nIf you don't know how to set \"User-Agent\" see here\n\nThe following methods are limited to Baidu super member users\n\nPrompt again The following methods are limited to Baidu super member users\n\nIt will only be useful if a member finishes modifying **`\"User-Agent\"`** (choose official and 302)\n\n...\n\nIf you use the webpage to modify the **`user-agent`** , you do not need to configure the `UA` of **Motrix** , and you can download it directly to Aria2\n\n* Motrix Download link： [Motrix Official website](https://motrix.app/) ， [Motrix-GitHub](https://github.com/agalwood/Motrix)\n\nalist\n\n  \n## **Upload Config**\n\nOfficial Documentation: [百度网盘开放平台 - 上传 - 能力说明](https://pan.baidu.com/union/doc/3ksg0s9ye)\n\n> Baidu Netdisk requires that each slice be uploaded within 30 seconds, so excessively high concurrency during file uploads may result in a significant number of failures.\n> \n> \n\n* `Use dynamic upload api` is enabled by default. AList will try to discover Baidu's current upload domain automatically and cache the result for about 1 hour.\n* `Upload API` is now the fallback endpoint when dynamic discovery fails. If it is blank or unavailable, AList falls back again to `https://d.pcs.baidu.com` .\n* `Upload Threads` : the number of slices to upload concurrently.\nValid range: `1` to `32` .\n* Baidu Netdisk does not allow uploading empty files.\n* AList will retry transient upload failures. If Baidu expires the current `uploadid` , AList recreates the upload session and retries the file from the beginning.\n* Custom Upload Slice Size: Allows you to specify the size of each slice. Note that there are limitations, and this feature is available to VIPs only.\n* Low Bandwidth Upload Mode: Attempts to address the frequent `Client.Timeout exceeded while awaiting headers` errors encountered in low upload bandwidth scenarios (e.g., residential broadband). When enabled, it uses the smallest possible slice size.\n\n  \n## **The default download method used**\n\n[Edit this page](https://github.com/alist-org/docs/edit/main/docs/guide/drivers/baidu.md)\n\nLast update: 5/7/2026, 2:52:20 AM\n\nContributors: Noah Hsu , 安稳 , Andy Hsu , Andy Hsu , 安稳 , Ljcbaby , anwen , JoaHuang , 千石\n\nPrev 123 Open (Open Platform) Next BaiduYun Share Link",
        "position": 6

If Baidu expires the current `uploadid` , AList recreates the upload session and retries the file from the beginning.\n* Custom Upload Slice Size: Allows you to specify the size of each slice. ### 提取内容摘要

**课程名称**：普通人怎么培养高层次思维

**核心观点**：
> 遇到复杂问题时，不急着下结论，能看清真正的问题，找到关键信息，分析原因和后果，作出相对靠谱的决定，并根据结果及时调整。

**关键方法**：
1. 学会用概率思考（不说"一定"，用数据验证）
2. 主动寻找反方（质疑自己的观点）
3. 设计低成本试验（先想清关键问题，再用行动换取反馈）
